import json
from psycopg.rows import dict_row

async def _one(conn, sql, params=()):
    async with conn.cursor(row_factory=dict_row) as cur:
        await cur.execute(sql, params)
        return await cur.fetchone()

async def _many(conn, sql, params=()):
    async with conn.cursor(row_factory=dict_row) as cur:
        await cur.execute(sql, params)
        return await cur.fetchall()

async def _lock_composition(conn,user_id,cid,revision):
    row=await _one(conn,"SELECT * FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL AND revision=%s FOR UPDATE",(cid,user_id,revision))
    if row:return row,None
    exists=await _one(conn,"SELECT id FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL",(cid,user_id))
    return None,"stale" if exists else "missing"

async def _destination(conn,user_id,parent_type,parent_id,active=True):
    tables={"content_item":"content_items","education_item":"education_items","tool_group":"tool_groups","interest":"interests","custom_entry":"custom_section_entries"}
    table=tables.get(parent_type)
    if not table:return None
    return await _one(conn,f"SELECT id FROM {table} WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s"+(" AND archived_at IS NULL" if active else ""),(parent_id,user_id))

async def _bullet_variant(conn,user_id,bullet_id,variant_id,active=True):
    clause=" AND b.archived_at IS NULL AND v.archived_at IS NULL" if active else ""
    return await _one(conn,"SELECT b.id bullet_id,b.section,b.parent_context,b.archived_at bullet_archived,v.id variant_id,v.body,v.updated_at,v.archived_at variant_archived FROM bullets b JOIN bullet_variants v ON v.bullet_id=b.id AND v.user_id=b.user_id WHERE b.id=%s AND v.id=%s AND b.user_id=%s"+clause,(bullet_id,variant_id,user_id))

async def _relationships(conn,cid):
    return await _many(conn,"""SELECT cb.id,cb.bullet_id,cb.variant_id,cb.destination_section,cb.destination_parent_type,cb.destination_parent_id,cb.sort_order,cb.wording_snapshot,cb.snapshot_source_updated_at,b.archived_at bullet_archived,v.archived_at variant_archived,v.body current_body,v.updated_at current_updated_at FROM composition_bullets cb LEFT JOIN bullets b ON b.id=cb.bullet_id LEFT JOIN bullet_variants v ON v.id=cb.variant_id WHERE cb.composition_id=%s ORDER BY cb.destination_section,cb.destination_parent_id,cb.sort_order,cb.id""",(cid,))

async def _snapshot(conn,row,resume=None):
    rels=await _relationships(conn,row["id"])
    return {"name":row["name"],"description":row.get("description","") ,"display_title":row.get("display_title","") ,"target_company":row.get("target_company","") ,"target_role":row.get("target_role","") ,"preferred_page_length":row.get("preferred_page_length",1),"date_format":row.get("date_format","long"),"section_sort_modes":row.get("section_sort_modes",{}),"resume":resume or row["render_payload"],"interest_layout":row.get("interest_layout","compact"),"section_order":row.get("section_order",[]),"bullet_placements":[{k:(str(v) if k.endswith("_id") and v is not None else v) for k,v in x.items() if k in {"id","bullet_id","variant_id","destination_section","destination_parent_type","destination_parent_id","sort_order","wording_snapshot","snapshot_source_updated_at"}} for x in rels]}

async def apply_placements(conn,user_id,cid,resume):
    rels=await _relationships(conn,cid)
    selected=await _many(conn,"SELECT section,item_id FROM composition_items WHERE composition_id=%s",(cid,))
    included={(x["section"],str(x["item_id"])) for x in selected}
    grouped={}
    for rel in rels: grouped.setdefault((rel["destination_section"],str(rel["destination_parent_id"])),[]).append(rel)
    for section,pid in included:
        key=section; rows=grouped.get((section,pid),[]); bodies=[x["wording_snapshot"] for x in rows]
        if section in {"projects","work_experience","leadership","education","tool_groups"}:
            for entry in resume.get(section,[]):
                if str(entry.get("id"))==pid: entry["bullets"]=bodies
        elif section=="personal_interests":
            for entry in resume.get("personal_interest_items",[]):
                if str(entry.get("id"))==pid: entry["bullets"]=bodies
        elif section=="custom_sections":
            for sec in resume.get("custom_sections",[]):
                for entry in sec.get("entries",[]):
                    if str(entry.get("id"))==pid: entry["bullets"]=bodies
    return resume
async def _commit(conn,user_id,locked,source):
    resume=await apply_placements(conn,user_id,locked["id"],locked["render_payload"])
    row=await _one(conn,"UPDATE resume_compositions SET render_payload=%s::jsonb,revision=revision+1,autosaved_at=now(),updated_at=now() WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s RETURNING *",(json.dumps(resume),locked["id"],user_id))
    snap=await _snapshot(conn,row,resume)
    await conn.execute("INSERT INTO composition_revisions(composition_id,user_id,revision,source,snapshot) VALUES(%s,%s,%s,%s,%s::jsonb)",(row["id"],user_id,row["revision"],source,json.dumps(snap,default=str)))
    return row

async def picker(conn,user_id,q,section,limit,offset):
    like=f"%{q.strip()}%"
    return await _many(conn,"""SELECT b.id,b.section,b.parent_context,b.created_at,b.updated_at,count(v.id) FILTER(WHERE v.archived_at IS NULL) variant_count,jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id',v.id,'name',v.name,'body',v.body,'is_default',v.is_default) ORDER BY v.is_default DESC,v.sort_order,v.id) FILTER(WHERE v.archived_at IS NULL) variants FROM bullets b JOIN bullet_variants v ON v.bullet_id=b.id AND v.user_id=b.user_id WHERE b.user_id=%s AND b.archived_at IS NULL AND v.archived_at IS NULL AND (%s='' OR b.section=%s) AND (v.body ILIKE %s OR COALESCE(b.parent_context,'') ILIKE %s) GROUP BY b.id ORDER BY min(lower(v.body)),b.id LIMIT %s OFFSET %s""",(user_id,section,section,like,like,limit,offset))

async def insert_placement(conn,user_id,cid,payload):
    async with conn.transaction():
        locked,error=await _lock_composition(conn,user_id,cid,payload.revision)
        if error:return None,error
        if not await _destination(conn,user_id,payload.destination_parent_type,payload.destination_parent_id):return None,"missing"
        if not await _one(conn,"SELECT 1 FROM composition_items WHERE composition_id=%s AND section=%s AND item_id=%s",(cid,payload.destination_section,payload.destination_parent_id)):return None,"missing"
        if await _one(conn,"SELECT 1 FROM composition_bullets WHERE composition_id=%s AND bullet_id=%s",(cid,payload.bullet_id)):return None,"duplicate"
        selected=await _bullet_variant(conn,user_id,payload.bullet_id,payload.variant_id,True)
        if not selected:return None,"missing"
        order=payload.sort_order
        if order is None:
            r=await _one(conn,"SELECT COALESCE(max(sort_order)+1,0) n FROM composition_bullets WHERE composition_id=%s AND destination_parent_type=%s AND destination_parent_id=%s",(cid,payload.destination_parent_type,payload.destination_parent_id));order=r["n"]
        await conn.execute("INSERT INTO composition_bullets(composition_id,bullet_id,variant_id,destination_section,destination_parent_type,destination_parent_id,sort_order,wording_snapshot,snapshot_source_updated_at) VALUES(%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)",(cid,payload.bullet_id,payload.variant_id,payload.destination_section,payload.destination_parent_type,payload.destination_parent_id,order,selected["body"],selected["updated_at"]))
        return await _commit(conn,user_id,locked,"bullet-placement-insert"),None

async def create_and_place(conn,user_id,cid,payload):
    async with conn.transaction():
        locked,error=await _lock_composition(conn,user_id,cid,payload.revision)
        if error:return None,error
        if not await _destination(conn,user_id,payload.destination_parent_type,payload.destination_parent_id):return None,"missing"
        parent_col={"content_item":"parent_id","education_item":"education_item_id","tool_group":"tool_group_id","interest":"interest_id","custom_entry":"custom_entry_id"}[payload.destination_parent_type]
        label=(await _one(conn,"SELECT phase3c1_parent_label(%s,%s,%s) label",(user_id,payload.destination_parent_type,payload.destination_parent_id)))["label"]
        bullet=await _one(conn,f"INSERT INTO bullets(user_id,{parent_col},section,parent_context,sort_order) VALUES(%s,NULL,%s,%s,(SELECT COALESCE(max(sort_order)+1,0) FROM bullets WHERE user_id=%s AND section=%s)) RETURNING id",(user_id,payload.library_section,label,user_id,payload.library_section))
        variant=await _one(conn,"INSERT INTO bullet_variants(user_id,bullet_id,name,body,is_default,sort_order) VALUES(%s,%s,%s,%s,true,0) RETURNING id,body,updated_at",(user_id,bullet["id"],payload.variant_name.strip(),payload.body.strip()))
        order=(await _one(conn,"SELECT COALESCE(max(sort_order)+1,0) n FROM composition_bullets WHERE composition_id=%s AND destination_parent_type=%s AND destination_parent_id=%s",(cid,payload.destination_parent_type,payload.destination_parent_id)))["n"]
        await conn.execute("INSERT INTO composition_bullets(composition_id,bullet_id,variant_id,destination_section,destination_parent_type,destination_parent_id,sort_order,wording_snapshot,snapshot_source_updated_at) VALUES(%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)",(cid,bullet["id"],variant["id"],payload.destination_section,payload.destination_parent_type,payload.destination_parent_id,order,variant["body"],variant["updated_at"]))
        return await _commit(conn,user_id,locked,"bullet-create-and-place"),None

async def remove_placement(conn,user_id,cid,placement_id,revision):
    async with conn.transaction():
        locked,error=await _lock_composition(conn,user_id,cid,revision)
        if error:return None,error
        row=await _one(conn,"DELETE FROM composition_bullets cb USING resume_compositions c WHERE cb.id=%s AND cb.composition_id=%s AND c.id=cb.composition_id AND c.user_id=%s RETURNING cb.destination_parent_type,cb.destination_parent_id",(placement_id,cid,user_id))
        if not row:return None,"missing"
        rows=await _many(conn,"SELECT id FROM composition_bullets WHERE composition_id=%s AND destination_parent_type=%s AND destination_parent_id=%s ORDER BY sort_order,id",(cid,row["destination_parent_type"],row["destination_parent_id"]))
        for i,x in enumerate(rows):await conn.execute("UPDATE composition_bullets SET sort_order=%s,updated_at=now() WHERE id=%s",(i,x["id"]))
        return await _commit(conn,user_id,locked,"bullet-placement-remove"),None

async def replace_placement(conn,user_id,cid,placement_id,payload):
    async with conn.transaction():
        locked,error=await _lock_composition(conn,user_id,cid,payload.revision)
        if error:return None,error
        selected=await _bullet_variant(conn,user_id,payload.bullet_id,payload.variant_id,True)
        if not selected:return None,"missing"
        row=await _one(conn,"UPDATE composition_bullets cb SET bullet_id=%s,variant_id=%s,wording_snapshot=%s,snapshot_source_updated_at=%s,updated_at=now() FROM resume_compositions c WHERE cb.id=%s AND cb.composition_id=%s AND c.id=cb.composition_id AND c.user_id=%s RETURNING cb.id",(payload.bullet_id,payload.variant_id,selected["body"],selected["updated_at"],placement_id,cid,user_id))
        if not row:return None,"missing"
        return await _commit(conn,user_id,locked,"bullet-placement-replace"),None

async def reorder_placements(conn,user_id,cid,payload):
    async with conn.transaction():
        locked,error=await _lock_composition(conn,user_id,cid,payload.revision)
        if error:return None,error
        rows=await _many(conn,"SELECT cb.id FROM composition_bullets cb JOIN resume_compositions c ON c.id=cb.composition_id WHERE cb.composition_id=%s AND c.user_id=%s AND cb.destination_section=%s AND cb.destination_parent_type=%s AND cb.destination_parent_id=%s ORDER BY cb.sort_order,cb.id FOR UPDATE",(cid,user_id,payload.destination_section,payload.destination_parent_type,payload.destination_parent_id))
        if {str(x["id"]) for x in rows}!={str(x) for x in payload.ids}:return None,"invalid"
        for i,rid in enumerate(payload.ids):await conn.execute("UPDATE composition_bullets SET sort_order=%s,updated_at=now() WHERE id=%s AND composition_id=%s",(i,rid,cid))
        return await _commit(conn,user_id,locked,"bullet-placement-reorder"),None

async def update_usage(conn,user_id,cid,placement_id,revision):
    async with conn.transaction():
        locked,error=await _lock_composition(conn,user_id,cid,revision)
        if error:return None,error
        row=await _one(conn,"UPDATE composition_bullets cb SET wording_snapshot=v.body,snapshot_source_updated_at=v.updated_at,updated_at=now() FROM bullet_variants v,resume_compositions c WHERE cb.id=%s AND cb.composition_id=%s AND c.id=cb.composition_id AND c.user_id=%s AND v.id=cb.variant_id RETURNING cb.id",(placement_id,cid,user_id))
        if not row:return None,"missing"
        return await _commit(conn,user_id,locked,"bullet-usage-update"),None

async def usage_rows(conn,user_id,cid):
    rows=await _many(conn,"""SELECT cb.*,v.name variant_name,(SELECT count(*) FROM bullet_variants av WHERE av.bullet_id=cb.bullet_id AND av.archived_at IS NULL) active_variant_count,CASE WHEN b.id IS NULL OR v.id IS NULL THEN 'missing' WHEN b.archived_at IS NOT NULL OR v.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN 'archived' WHEN cb.wording_snapshot IS DISTINCT FROM v.body OR cb.snapshot_source_updated_at IS DISTINCT FROM v.updated_at THEN 'outdated' ELSE 'current' END usage_status FROM composition_bullets cb JOIN resume_compositions c ON c.id=cb.composition_id LEFT JOIN bullets b ON b.id=cb.bullet_id LEFT JOIN bullet_variants v ON v.id=cb.variant_id WHERE cb.composition_id=%s AND c.user_id=%s ORDER BY cb.destination_section,cb.destination_parent_id,cb.sort_order,cb.id""",(cid,user_id))
    return rows

from psycopg.rows import dict_row
async def many(c,q,p=()):
 async with c.cursor(row_factory=dict_row) as x: await x.execute(q,p);return await x.fetchall()
async def one(c,q,p=()):
 x=await many(c,q,p);return x[0] if x else None
def norm(s):
 d=s.strip()
 if not d: raise ValueError("Tag name cannot be blank")
 return d,d.casefold()
async def counts(c,u):
 x=await many(c,"SELECT section,count(*) count FROM bullets WHERE user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL GROUP BY section",(u,));z={a['section']:a['count'] for a in x};z['all']=sum(z.values());return z
async def tags(c,u,q=""):
 return await many(c,"SELECT id,display_name,normalized_name,created_at,updated_at FROM library_tags WHERE user_id=%s AND normalized_name LIKE %s ORDER BY normalized_name,id",(u,f"%{q.strip().casefold()}%"))
async def upsert_tag(c,u,n):
 d,k=norm(n);return await one(c,"INSERT INTO library_tags(user_id,display_name,normalized_name) VALUES(%s,%s,%s) ON CONFLICT(user_id,normalized_name) DO UPDATE SET updated_at=library_tags.updated_at RETURNING *",(u,d,k))
async def items(c,u,q,status,section,tag_ids,page,size):
 w=["b.user_id=%s"];p=[u]
 if status=="active":w+=["b.archived_at IS NULL"]
 if status=="archived":w+=["b.archived_at IS NOT NULL"]
 if section:w+=["b.section=%s"];p+=[section]
 if q.strip():
  like=f"%{q.strip()}%";w+=["(d.body ILIKE %s OR b.parent_context ILIKE %s OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM library_bullet_tags bt JOIN library_tags t ON t.id=bt.tag_id WHERE bt.bullet_id=b.id AND t.display_name ILIKE %s))"];p += [like,like,like]
 if tag_ids:w+=["b.id IN(SELECT bullet_id FROM library_bullet_tags WHERE tag_id=ANY(%s) GROUP BY bullet_id HAVING count(DISTINCT tag_id)=%s)"];p += [tag_ids,len(tag_ids)]
 pred=" AND ".join(w);total=(await one(c,f"SELECT count(*) total FROM bullets b LEFT JOIN LATERAL(SELECT body FROM bullet_variants v WHERE v.bullet_id=b.id ORDER BY v.is_default DESC,v.sort_order,v.id LIMIT 1)d ON true WHERE {pred}",p))["total"]
 rows=await many(c,f"""SELECT b.id,b.section,b.parent_id,b.parent_context,b.archived_at,b.created_at,b.updated_at,COALESCE(d.body,'') body,(SELECT count(*) FROM bullet_variants v WHERE v.bullet_id=b.id) variant_count,COALESCE((SELECT json_agg(json_build_object('id',t.id,'display_name',t.display_name) ORDER BY t.normalized_name,t.id) FROM library_bullet_tags bt JOIN library_tags t ON t.id=bt.tag_id WHERE bt.bullet_id=b.id),'[]'::json) tags FROM bullets b LEFT JOIN LATERAL(SELECT body FROM bullet_variants v WHERE v.bullet_id=b.id AND v.archived_at IS NULL ORDER BY v.is_default DESC,v.sort_order,v.id LIMIT 1)d ON true WHERE {pred} ORDER BY lower(COALESCE(d.body,'')),b.updated_at DESC,b.id LIMIT %s OFFSET %s""",p+[size,(page-1)*size])
 pages=(total+size-1)//size;return dict(items=rows,page=page,page_size=size,total_items=total,total_pages=pages,has_previous=page>1,has_next=page<pages)
async def create_item(c,u,x):
 async with c.transaction():
  b=await one(c,"INSERT INTO bullets(user_id,parent_id,section,parent_context,sort_order) VALUES(%s,NULL,%s,%s,(SELECT COALESCE(max(sort_order)+1,0) FROM bullets WHERE user_id=%s AND section=%s)) RETURNING id",(u,x.section,x.parent_context.strip() or None,u,x.section));v=await one(c,"INSERT INTO bullet_variants(user_id,bullet_id,name,body,is_default,sort_order) VALUES(%s,%s,%s,%s,true,0) RETURNING id",(u,b['id'],x.variant_name.strip(),x.body.strip()))
  for n in x.tags:
   t=await upsert_tag(c,u,n);await c.execute("INSERT INTO library_bullet_tags VALUES(%s,%s,now()) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",(b['id'],t['id']))
  return {'id':b['id'],'variant_id':v['id']}
async def replace_tags(c,u,bid,names):
 async with c.transaction():
  if not await one(c,"SELECT id FROM bullets WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s FOR UPDATE",(bid,u)):return None
  await c.execute("DELETE FROM library_bullet_tags WHERE bullet_id=%s",(bid,))
  for n in names:
   t=await upsert_tag(c,u,n);await c.execute("INSERT INTO library_bullet_tags(bullet_id,tag_id) VALUES(%s,%s) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",(bid,t['id']))
  return {'id':bid}
async def bulk(c,u,ids,action,names,section):
 async with c.transaction():
  own=await many(c,"SELECT id FROM bullets WHERE user_id=%s AND id=ANY(%s) FOR UPDATE",(u,ids))
  if {x['id'] for x in own}!=set(ids):return None
  if action in ('archive','restore'):await c.execute(f"UPDATE bullets SET archived_at={'now()' if action=='archive' else 'NULL'},updated_at=now() WHERE user_id=%s AND id=ANY(%s)",(u,ids))
  elif action=='move-section':await c.execute("UPDATE bullets SET section=%s,updated_at=now() WHERE user_id=%s AND id=ANY(%s)",(section,u,ids))
  else:
   tids=[(await upsert_tag(c,u,n))['id'] for n in names]
   if action=='add-tags':
    for bid in ids:
     for tid in tids:await c.execute("INSERT INTO library_bullet_tags(bullet_id,tag_id) VALUES(%s,%s) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",(bid,tid))
   else:await c.execute("DELETE FROM library_bullet_tags WHERE bullet_id=ANY(%s) AND tag_id=ANY(%s)",(ids,tids))
  return {'succeeded':ids,'failed':[]}
async def usages(c,u,bid):
 return await many(c,"""SELECT cb.id placement_id,c.id composition_id,c.name resume_name,cb.destination_section section,cb.destination_parent_type,cb.destination_parent_id,cb.variant_id,v.name variant_name,CASE WHEN b.archived_at IS NOT NULL OR v.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN 'Archived' WHEN cb.wording_snapshot IS NULL THEN 'Missing' WHEN cb.snapshot_source_updated_at=v.updated_at THEN 'Current' ELSE 'Outdated' END status FROM composition_bullets cb JOIN resume_compositions c ON c.id=cb.composition_id JOIN bullets b ON b.id=cb.bullet_id LEFT JOIN bullet_variants v ON v.id=cb.variant_id WHERE cb.bullet_id=%s AND c.user_id=%s ORDER BY lower(c.name),c.id,cb.id""",(bid,u))


async def detail(c,u,bid):
 item=await one(c,"""SELECT b.id,b.section,b.parent_context,b.archived_at,b.created_at,b.updated_at,COALESCE(d.body,'') body FROM bullets b LEFT JOIN LATERAL(SELECT body FROM bullet_variants v WHERE v.bullet_id=b.id ORDER BY v.is_default DESC,v.sort_order,v.id LIMIT 1)d ON true WHERE b.id=%s AND b.user_id=%s""",(bid,u))
 if not item:return None
 item['variants']=await many(c,"SELECT id,name,body,is_default,sort_order,archived_at,created_at,updated_at FROM bullet_variants WHERE bullet_id=%s AND user_id=%s ORDER BY sort_order,id",(bid,u))
 item['tags']=await many(c,"SELECT t.id,t.display_name,t.normalized_name FROM library_bullet_tags bt JOIN library_tags t ON t.id=bt.tag_id WHERE bt.bullet_id=%s AND t.user_id=%s ORDER BY t.normalized_name,t.id",(bid,u))
 item['usage']=await usages(c,u,bid)
 item['parent_id']=item.get('parent_id')
 item['parent_type']='content_item' if item.get('parent_id') else None
 item['parent_options']=await parent_options(c,u)
 return item

async def parent_options(c,u):
 return await many(c,"""SELECT id,'content_item' type,CASE kind WHEN 'employment' THEN 'experience' ELSE kind END section,CASE WHEN kind='project' THEN COALESCE(name,'') ELSE concat_ws(' — ',organization,title) END label FROM content_items WHERE user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL AND kind IN ('employment','project','leadership') ORDER BY kind,lower(CASE WHEN kind='project' THEN COALESCE(name,'') ELSE concat_ws(' — ',organization,title) END),id""",(u,))
async def update_parent(c,u,bid,parent_id,parent_type):
 async with c.transaction():
  bullet=await one(c,"SELECT id,section,parent_id FROM bullets WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s FOR UPDATE",(bid,u))
  if not bullet:return None,'missing'
  if parent_id is None:
   row=await one(c,"UPDATE bullets SET parent_id=NULL,parent_context=NULL,updated_at=now() WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s RETURNING id,parent_id,parent_context",(bid,u));return row,None
  if parent_type!='content_item':return None,'invalid'
  expected={'experience':'employment','projects':'project','leadership':'leadership'}.get(bullet['section'])
  parent=await one(c,"SELECT id,kind,CASE WHEN kind='project' THEN COALESCE(name,'') ELSE concat_ws(' — ',organization,title) END label FROM content_items WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL",(parent_id,u))
  if not parent:return None,'missing'
  if not expected or parent['kind']!=expected:return None,'invalid'
  row=await one(c,"UPDATE bullets SET parent_id=%s,parent_context=%s,updated_at=now() WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s RETURNING id,parent_id,parent_context",(parent_id,parent['label'],bid,u));return row,None

import calendar,re
DATE_FORMATS={"year","numeric","short","long"}
def validate_dates(start_year=None,start_month=None,end_year=None,end_month=None,is_current=False):
 if start_month and not start_year: raise ValueError("Start month requires a start year.")
 if end_month and not end_year: raise ValueError("End month requires an end year.")
 if is_current and (end_year or end_month): raise ValueError("Current entries cannot have an end date.")
 if start_year and end_year and (end_year,end_month or 12)<(start_year,start_month or 1): raise ValueError("End date cannot precede start date.")
def format_point(year,month,style):
 if not year:return ""
 if style=="year" or not month:return str(year)
 if style=="numeric":return f"{month:02d}/{year}"
 return f"{calendar.month_abbr[month] if style=='short' else calendar.month_name[month]} {year}"
def display_date(row,style="long"):
 override=(row.get("date_label") or row.get("display_date") or "").strip()
 if override:return override
 start=format_point(row.get("start_year"),row.get("start_month"),style)
 end="Present" if row.get("is_current") else format_point(row.get("end_year"),row.get("end_month"),style)
 return " - ".join(x for x in (start,end) if x)
def chronological_key(row):
 structured=bool(row.get("start_year") or row.get("end_year") or row.get("is_current"))
 if not structured:return (1,0,0,row.get("manual_order",0),str(row.get("id","")))
 end=999912 if row.get("is_current") else (row.get("end_year") or 0)*100+(row.get("end_month") or 12)
 start=(row.get("start_year") or 0)*100+(row.get("start_month") or 1)
 return (0,-int(bool(row.get("is_current"))),-end,-start,row.get("manual_order",0),str(row.get("id","")))
def normalize_company(value):return re.sub(r"\s+"," ",(value or "").strip()).casefold()
def month_value(year,month,boundary):return year*12+(month or boundary)-1 if year else None
def continuous(previous,current):
 if normalize_company(previous.get("organization"))!=normalize_company(current.get("organization")):return False
 prior_end=month_value(previous.get("end_year"),previous.get("end_month"),12)
 if previous.get("is_current"):prior_end=999999
 current_start=month_value(current.get("start_year"),current.get("start_month"),1)
 current_end=month_value(current.get("end_year"),current.get("end_month"),12) or (999999 if current.get("is_current") else None)
 prior_start=month_value(previous.get("start_year"),previous.get("start_month"),1)
 if None in (prior_end,current_start,current_end,prior_start):return False
 return current_start<=prior_end+1 and prior_start<=current_end+1
def preprocess_resume(resume,sort_modes=None,date_format="long"):
 data={**resume};modes=sort_modes or {}
 for key in ("projects","work_experience","leadership"):
  rows=[dict(x) for x in data.get(key,[])]
  for row in rows:
   rendered=display_date(row,date_format)
   if rendered or not row.get("date"):
    row["date"]=rendered
  if modes.get(key,"automatic")=="automatic":rows=sorted(rows,key=chronological_key)
  if key=="work_experience":
   for i,row in enumerate(rows):row["suppress_organization"]=i>0 and continuous(rows[i-1],row)
  data[key]=rows
 education=[]
 for item in data.get("education",[]):
  row=dict(item)
  override=(row.get("display_date") or "").strip()
  if override:
   row["graduation_date"]=override
  elif row.get("graduation_year"):
   row["graduation_date"]=format_point(row.get("graduation_year"),row.get("graduation_month"),date_format)
  education.append(row)
 data["education"]=education
 custom=[]
 for section in data.get("custom_sections",[]):
  sec=dict(section); entries=[]
  for item in sec.get("entries",[]):
   row=dict(item); rendered=display_date(row,date_format)
   if rendered or not row.get("date"): row["date"]=rendered
   entries.append(row)
  sec["entries"]=entries; custom.append(sec)
 data["custom_sections"]=custom
 return data

import json
from psycopg.rows import dict_row
async def one(c,q,p=()):
 async with c.cursor(row_factory=dict_row) as x:await x.execute(q,p);return await x.fetchone()
async def update_preferences(c,u,cid,req):
 async with c.transaction():
  row=await one(c,"UPDATE resume_compositions SET preferred_page_length=%s,date_format=%s,section_sort_modes=%s::jsonb,revision=revision+1,updated_at=now() WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL AND revision=%s RETURNING *",(req.preferred_page_length,req.date_format,json.dumps(req.section_sort_modes),cid,u,req.revision))
  if not row:
   exists=await one(c,"SELECT id FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL",(cid,u));return None,"stale" if exists else "missing"
  await c.execute("INSERT INTO composition_revisions(composition_id,user_id,revision,source,snapshot) VALUES(%s,%s,%s,'phase2l-preferences',%s::jsonb)",(cid,u,row['revision'],json.dumps({'resume':row['render_payload'],'preferred_page_length':row['preferred_page_length'],'date_format':row['date_format'],'section_sort_modes':row['section_sort_modes']})))
  return row,None

from copy import deepcopy
from psycopg.rows import dict_row
from .phase2l import preprocess_resume

RENDERER_CONTRACT_VERSION = "2m.1"

async def one(conn, sql, params=()):
    async with conn.cursor(row_factory=dict_row) as cur:
        await cur.execute(sql, params)
        return await cur.fetchone()

async def materialize_composition(conn, user_id, composition_id, revision=None):
    params=[composition_id,user_id]
    revision_clause=""
    if revision is not None:
        revision_clause=" AND revision=%s"; params.append(revision)
    row=await one(conn, """SELECT id,name,revision,render_payload,preferred_page_length,date_format,section_sort_modes,section_order,interest_layout,archived_at FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL"""+revision_clause, tuple(params))
    if not row:
        exists=await one(conn,"SELECT id FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=%s AND user_id=%s AND archived_at IS NULL",(composition_id,user_id))
        return None, "stale" if exists and revision is not None else "missing"
    resume=deepcopy(row["render_payload"])
    resume["section_order"]=row.get("section_order") or resume.get("section_order",[])
    resume["interest_layout"]=row.get("interest_layout") or resume.get("interest_layout","compact")
    resume=preprocess_resume(resume,row.get("section_sort_modes") or {},row.get("date_format") or "long")
    # Hidden state is authoritative in section_order and never mutates Library data.
    resume["section_order"]=[x for x in resume.get("section_order",[]) if not str(x).startswith("hidden:")]
    return {"composition_id":str(row["id"]),"revision":row["revision"],"name":row["name"],"preferred_page_count":row["preferred_page_length"],"renderer_contract_version":RENDERER_CONTRACT_VERSION,"section_sort_modes":row.get("section_sort_modes") or {},"date_format":row.get("date_format") or "long","resume":resume},None


async def materialize_revision(conn,user_id,composition_id,revision_id):
    row=await one(conn,"""SELECT r.snapshot,c.preferred_page_length,c.date_format,c.section_sort_modes FROM composition_revisions r JOIN resume_compositions c ON c.id=r.composition_id WHERE r.id=%s AND r.composition_id=%s AND r.user_id=%s AND c.user_id=%s""",(revision_id,composition_id,user_id,user_id))
    if not row:return None,"missing"
    snapshot=row["snapshot"] or {}; resume=deepcopy(snapshot.get("resume") or {})
    resume["section_order"]=snapshot.get("section_order",resume.get("section_order",[]));resume["interest_layout"]=snapshot.get("interest_layout",resume.get("interest_layout","compact"))
    modes=snapshot.get("section_sort_modes",row["section_sort_modes"] or {});date_format=snapshot.get("date_format",row["date_format"] or "long")
    resume=preprocess_resume(resume,modes,date_format);resume["section_order"]=[x for x in resume.get("section_order",[]) if not str(x).startswith("hidden:")]
    return {"resume":resume,"section_sort_modes":modes,"date_format":date_format,"renderer_contract_version":RENDERER_CONTRACT_VERSION},None

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/app/phase2m_repository.py
================================================================================

BEGIN FILE: backend/app/render.py

from pathlib import Path
from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape
from .schemas import Resume
from .phase2l import preprocess_resume

TEMPLATE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "templates"
env = Environment(
    loader=FileSystemLoader(TEMPLATE_DIR), autoescape=select_autoescape(["html", "xml"])
)


def render_resume(resume: Resume | dict, measure: bool = True, renderer_contract_version: str = "legacy", sort_modes: dict | None = None, date_format: str = "long") -> str:
    data = resume.model_dump() if isinstance(resume, Resume) else resume
    return env.get_template("resume.html").render(
        resume=preprocess_resume(data, sort_modes, date_format), measure=measure,
        renderer_contract_version=renderer_contract_version,
    )

END FILE: backend/app/render.py

BEGIN FILE: backend/app/schemas.py

from typing import Literal
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class Header(BaseModel):
    name: str
    phone: str = ""
    email: str = ""
    linkedin_label: str = ""
    linkedin_url: str = ""


class Education(BaseModel):
    id: str | None = None
    institution: str
    location: str = ""
    degree: str = ""
    graduation_date: str = ""


class ToolGroup(BaseModel):
    id: str | None = None
    name: str
    items: list[str]
    bullets: list[str] = []


class Project(BaseModel):
    id: str | None = None
    name: str
    subtitle: str = ""
    date: str = ""
    bullets: list[str]


class Experience(BaseModel):
    id: str | None = None
    organization: str
    location: str = ""
    title: str
    date: str = ""
    bullets: list[str]


class CustomEntry(BaseModel):
    id: str | None = None
    primary_text: str
    secondary_text: str | None = None
    location: str | None = None
    date: str | None = None
    bullets: list[str] = []


class CustomSection(BaseModel):
    id: str | None = None
    title: str
    layout: Literal["list", "compact", "dated", "experience"] = "list"
    entries: list[CustomEntry] = []


class Resume(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    header: Header
    summary: str = ""
    education: list[Education] = []
    tool_groups: list[ToolGroup] = []
    projects: list[Project] = []
    work_experience: list[Experience] = []
    leadership: list[Experience] = []
    personal_interests: list[str] = []
    personal_interest_items: list[dict] = []
    interest_bullets: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    section_order: list[str] = [
        "summary",
        "education",
        "tools",
        "projects",
        "work_experience",
        "leadership",
        "personal_interests",
    ]
    interest_layout: Literal["compact", "lines"] = "compact"
    custom_sections: list[CustomSection] = []

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/app/schemas.py

    <script>
      // Contract markers: document.fonts.ready; data-page-index; structured page descriptors.

      (async () => {
        const state = {
          type: "resume-pages",
          state: "loading",
          contract: "{{renderer_contract_version}}",
        };
        try {
          if (document.fonts) {
            state.state = "waiting-for-fonts";
            await document.fonts.ready;
          }
          state.state = "measuring";
          const source = document.getElementById("source"),
            pages = document.getElementById("pages");
          let page;
          const newPage = () => {
            page = document.createElement("section");
            page.className = "resume-page";
            page.dataset.pageIndex = String(pages.children.length);
            const c = document.createElement("div");
            c.className = "page-content";
            page.append(c);
            pages.append(page);
            return c;
          };
          let content = newPage();
          const fits = () => content.scrollHeight <= content.clientHeight + 1;
          for (const unit of [...source.children]) {
            const clone = unit.cloneNode(true);
            content.append(clone);
            if (fits()) continue;
            clone.remove();
            if (unit.classList.contains("section")) {
              const title = unit.querySelector(".section-title");
              const items = [...unit.querySelectorAll(":scope > .item")];
              if (items.length) {
                content = newPage();
                const section = unit.cloneNode(false);
                if (title) section.append(title.cloneNode(true));
                content.append(section);
                for (const item of items) {
                  const ic = item.cloneNode(true);
                  section.append(ic);
                  if (fits()) continue;
                  ic.remove();
                  content = newPage();
                  const next = unit.cloneNode(false);
                  if (title) {
                    const h = title.cloneNode(true);
                    next.append(h);
                    const mark = document.createElement("div");
                    mark.className = "continuation";
                    mark.textContent = "Continued";
                    next.append(mark);
                  }
                  content.append(next);
                  next.append(ic);
                  if (!fits()) {
                    ic.dataset.oversized = "true";
                    ic.style.breakInside = "auto";
                  }
                }
                continue;
              }
            }
            content = newPage();
            content.append(clone);
          }
          source.remove();
          const descriptors = [...pages.children].map((p, i) => ({
            index: i,
            section_ids: [...p.querySelectorAll("[data-section-id]")].map(
              (x) => x.dataset.sectionId,
            ),
            item_ids: [...p.querySelectorAll("[data-item-id]")].map(
              (x) => x.dataset.itemId,
            ),
            bullet_ids: [...p.querySelectorAll("[data-bullet-id]")].map(
              (x) => x.dataset.bulletId,
            ),
            oversized_item_ids: [
              ...p.querySelectorAll("[data-oversized=true]"),
            ].map((x) => x.dataset.itemId),
          }));
          state.state = "measured";
          state.actual_page_count = descriptors.length;
          state.pages = descriptors;
          const showPage = (index) => { const safe=Math.max(0,Math.min(descriptors.length-1,Number(index)||0)); [...pages.children].forEach((p,i)=>p.dataset.activePage=String(i===safe)); };
          {% if measure %}
          showPage(0);
          addEventListener("message",event=>{if(event.data?.type==="resume-show-page")showPage(event.data.pageIndex)});
          {% endif %}
          parent.postMessage(state, "*");
        } catch (e) {
          parent.postMessage(
            {
              ...state,
              state: "measurement-error",
              error_code: "PAGINATION_FAILED",
              message: "Resume measurement failed.",
            },
            "*",
          );
        }
      })();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

BEGIN FILE: backend/migrations/001_phase1b_master_library.sql

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;

CREATE TABLE app_users (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  external_id text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  username text NOT NULL,
  display_name text NOT NULL,
  email text,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE profiles (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES app_users (id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  phone text NOT NULL,
  email text NOT NULL,
  linkedin_label text NOT NULL,
  linkedin_url text NOT NULL,
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE summary_variants (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  body text NOT NULL,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, name)
);

CREATE TABLE education_items (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  institution text NOT NULL,
  location text NOT NULL,
  degree text NOT NULL,
  graduation_date text NOT NULL,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE tool_groups (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, name)
);

CREATE TABLE tools (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  group_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES tool_groups (id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (group_id, name)
);

CREATE TABLE content_items (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  kind text NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('project', 'employment', 'leadership')),
  name text,
  subtitle text,
  organization text,
  location text,
  title text,
  date_label text NOT NULL,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CHECK (
    (
      kind = 'project'
      AND name IS NOT NULL
    )
    OR (
      kind <> 'project'
      AND organization IS NOT NULL
      AND title IS NOT NULL
    )
  )
);

CREATE TABLE bullets (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  parent_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES content_items (id),
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE bullet_variants (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  bullet_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES bullets (id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  body text NOT NULL,
  is_default boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (bullet_id, name)
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX one_default_variant_per_bullet ON bullet_variants (bullet_id)
WHERE
  is_default
  AND archived_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE interests (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  body text NOT NULL,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE resume_compositions (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  name text NOT NULL,
  summary_variant_id uuid REFERENCES summary_variants (id),
  render_payload jsonb NOT NULL,
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, name)
);

CREATE TABLE composition_items (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  composition_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES resume_compositions (id),
  section text NOT NULL,
  item_id uuid NOT NULL,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  UNIQUE (composition_id, section, item_id)
);

CREATE TABLE composition_bullets (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  composition_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES resume_compositions (id),
  bullet_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES bullets (id),
  variant_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES bullet_variants (id),
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  UNIQUE (composition_id, bullet_id)
);

CREATE TABLE seed_imports (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  seed_key text NOT NULL,
  seed_sha256 text NOT NULL CHECK (length(seed_sha256) = 64),
  composition_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES resume_compositions (id),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, seed_key)
);

CREATE INDEX content_items_user_kind_order ON content_items (user_id, kind, sort_order);

CREATE INDEX bullets_parent_order ON bullets (parent_id, sort_order);

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/migrations/001_phase1b_master_library.sql
================================================================================

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: backend/migrations/003_phase1d_ownership_guards.sql
================================================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_matching_user_ownership () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE expected_user uuid;
BEGIN
  IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'tools' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM tool_groups WHERE id=NEW.group_id;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'bullets' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM content_items WHERE id=NEW.parent_id;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'bullet_variants' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM bullets WHERE id=NEW.bullet_id;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'seed_imports' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=NEW.composition_id;
  END IF;
  IF expected_user IS NULL OR expected_user <> NEW.user_id THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503'; END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

CREATE TRIGGER tools_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON tools FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_matching_user_ownership ();

CREATE TRIGGER bullets_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON bullets FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_matching_user_ownership ();

CREATE TRIGGER bullet_variants_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON bullet_variants FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_matching_user_ownership ();

CREATE TRIGGER seed_imports_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON seed_imports FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_matching_user_ownership ();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_composition_selection_ownership () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE composition_user uuid; selected_user uuid; bullet_user uuid;
BEGIN
  SELECT user_id INTO composition_user FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=NEW.composition_id;
  IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'composition_items' THEN
    IF NEW.section='education' THEN SELECT user_id INTO selected_user FROM education_items WHERE id=NEW.item_id;
    ELSIF NEW.section='tool_groups' THEN SELECT user_id INTO selected_user FROM tool_groups WHERE id=NEW.item_id;
    ELSIF NEW.section IN ('projects','work_experience','leadership') THEN SELECT user_id INTO selected_user FROM content_items WHERE id=NEW.item_id;
    ELSIF NEW.section='personal_interests' THEN SELECT user_id INTO selected_user FROM interests WHERE id=NEW.item_id;
    END IF;
  ELSE
    SELECT user_id INTO selected_user FROM bullet_variants WHERE id=NEW.variant_id AND bullet_id=NEW.bullet_id;
    SELECT user_id INTO bullet_user FROM bullets WHERE id=NEW.bullet_id;
    IF bullet_user IS DISTINCT FROM selected_user THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'bullet variant ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503'; END IF;
  END IF;
  IF composition_user IS NULL OR selected_user IS NULL OR composition_user <> selected_user THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'composition ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503'; END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

CREATE TRIGGER composition_items_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON composition_items FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_composition_selection_ownership ();

CREATE TRIGGER composition_bullets_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON composition_bullets FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_composition_selection_ownership ();

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/migrations/003_phase1d_ownership_guards.sql
================================================================================

CREATE TABLE custom_sections (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  title text NOT NULL,
  layout text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'list' CHECK (
    layout IN ('list', 'compact', 'dated', 'experience')
  ),
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, title)
);

CREATE TABLE custom_section_entries (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  section_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES custom_sections (id),
  primary_text text NOT NULL,
  secondary_text text,
  location text,
  date_label text,
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  archived_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE composition_custom_sections (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  composition_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES resume_compositions (id),
  section_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES custom_sections (id),
  sort_order integer NOT NULL CHECK (sort_order >= 0),
  UNIQUE (composition_id, section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE composition_revisions (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  composition_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES resume_compositions (id),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  revision integer NOT NULL,
  source text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'autosave',
  note text,
  snapshot jsonb NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (composition_id, revision)
);

CREATE INDEX composition_revisions_lookup ON composition_revisions (composition_id, revision DESC);

CREATE INDEX summary_search ON summary_variants (user_id, lower(name));

CREATE INDEX content_search ON content_items (
  user_id,
  kind,
  lower(COALESCE(name, organization, ''))
);

CREATE INDEX custom_sections_order ON custom_sections (user_id, sort_order);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_phase2_ownership () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$ DECLARE expected uuid; BEGIN
 IF TG_TABLE_NAME='custom_section_entries' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected FROM custom_sections WHERE id=NEW.section_id;
 ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='composition_custom_sections' THEN SELECT c.user_id INTO expected FROM resume_compositions c JOIN custom_sections s ON s.id=NEW.section_id AND s.user_id=c.user_id WHERE c.id=NEW.composition_id;
 ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='composition_revisions' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=NEW.composition_id;
 END IF;
 IF expected IS NULL OR (TG_TABLE_NAME<>'composition_custom_sections' AND expected<>NEW.user_id) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503'; END IF; RETURN NEW; END $$;

CREATE TRIGGER custom_entry_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON custom_section_entries FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_phase2_ownership ();

CREATE TRIGGER composition_custom_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON composition_custom_sections FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_phase2_ownership ();

CREATE TRIGGER composition_revision_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON composition_revisions FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_phase2_ownership ();

INSERT INTO
  composition_revisions (
    composition_id,
    user_id,
    revision,
    source,
    snapshot
  )
SELECT
  id,
  user_id,
  revision,
  'migration',
  jsonb_build_object(
    'name',
    name,
    'description',
    description,
    'resume',
    render_payload,
    'interest_layout',
    interest_layout,
    'section_order',
    section_order
  )
FROM
  resume_compositions
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

BEGIN FILE: backend/migrations/007_phase2c_bullet_selections.sql
================================================================================

-- Backfill only deterministic seed composition selections whose rendered parent and bullet wording match.
WITH
  visible AS (
    SELECT
      si.composition_id,
      si.user_id,
      x.id parent_id,
      b.id bullet_id,
      v.id variant_id,
      b.sort_order,
      CASE x.kind
        WHEN 'project' THEN 'projects'
        WHEN 'employment' THEN 'work_experience'
        ELSE 'leadership'
      END section,
      CASE
        WHEN x.kind = 'project' THEN x.name
        ELSE x.organization
      END parent_name,
      CASE
        WHEN x.kind = 'project' THEN x.subtitle
        ELSE x.title
      END parent_secondary,
      x.date_label,
      v.body
    FROM
      seed_imports si
      JOIN content_items x ON x.user_id = si.user_id
      JOIN bullets b ON b.parent_id = x.id
      AND b.user_id = si.user_id
      JOIN bullet_variants v ON v.bullet_id = b.id
      AND v.user_id = si.user_id
      AND v.is_default
      AND v.archived_at IS NULL
  ),
  exact_matches AS (
    SELECT
      visible.*
    FROM
      visible
      JOIN resume_compositions c ON c.id = visible.composition_id
      AND c.user_id = visible.user_id
    WHERE
      EXISTS (
        SELECT
          1
        FROM
          jsonb_array_elements(
            COALESCE(c.render_payload -> visible.section, '[]'::jsonb)
          ) item,
          jsonb_array_elements_text(COALESCE(item -> 'bullets', '[]'::jsonb)) wording
        WHERE
          item ->> 'date' = visible.date_label
          AND (
            item ->> 'name' = visible.parent_name
            OR item ->> 'organization' = visible.parent_name
          )
          AND (
            item ->> 'subtitle' = visible.parent_secondary
            OR item ->> 'title' = visible.parent_secondary
          )
          AND wording = visible.body
      )
  )
INSERT INTO
  composition_bullets (composition_id, bullet_id, variant_id, sort_order)
SELECT
  composition_id,
  bullet_id,
  variant_id,
  sort_order
FROM
  exact_matches
ON CONFLICT (composition_id, bullet_id) DO NOTHING;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS composition_bullets_variant_lookup ON composition_bullets (variant_id, composition_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS bullet_variants_bullet_order ON bullet_variants (bullet_id, sort_order, id);

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/migrations/007_phase2c_bullet_selections.sql
================================================================================

BEGIN FILE: backend/migrations/010_phase2j_reusable_bullet_placements.sql
================================================================================

-- Phase 2J: canonical reusable bullets and resume-specific placements.
-- Additive and non-destructive. Existing identifiers and render payloads remain valid.
ALTER TABLE bullets
ALTER COLUMN parent_id
DROP NOT NULL,
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS section text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'experience',
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS parent_context text;

UPDATE bullets b
SET
  section = CASE ci.kind
    WHEN 'project' THEN 'projects'
    WHEN 'employment' THEN 'experience'
    ELSE 'leadership'
  END
FROM
  content_items ci
WHERE
  ci.id = b.parent_id;

ALTER TABLE bullets
ADD CONSTRAINT bullets_section_check CHECK (
  section IN (
    'experience',
    'projects',
    'education',
    'leadership',
    'skills',
    'interests',
    'custom'
  )
);

ALTER TABLE composition_bullets
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS destination_section text,
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS destination_parent_type text,
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS destination_parent_id uuid,
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS wording_snapshot text,
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS snapshot_source_updated_at timestamptz,
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now();

ALTER TABLE composition_bullets
ADD CONSTRAINT composition_bullets_destination_section_check CHECK (
  destination_section IS NULL
  OR destination_section IN (
    'projects',
    'work_experience',
    'education',
    'leadership',
    'tool_groups',
    'personal_interests',
    'custom_sections'
  )
),
ADD CONSTRAINT composition_bullets_destination_type_check CHECK (
  destination_parent_type IS NULL
  OR destination_parent_type IN (
    'content_item',
    'education_item',
    'tool_group',
    'interest',
    'custom_entry'
  )
);

-- Existing composition selections are deterministic because the historical bullet parent is explicit.
UPDATE composition_bullets cb
SET
  destination_section = CASE ci.kind
    WHEN 'project' THEN 'projects'
    WHEN 'employment' THEN 'work_experience'
    ELSE 'leadership'
  END,
  destination_parent_type = 'content_item',
  destination_parent_id = b.parent_id,
  wording_snapshot = v.body,
  snapshot_source_updated_at = v.updated_at,
  updated_at = now()
FROM
  bullets b
  JOIN content_items ci ON ci.id = b.parent_id
  AND ci.user_id = b.user_id
  JOIN bullet_variants v ON v.bullet_id = b.id
  AND v.user_id = b.user_id
WHERE
  cb.bullet_id = b.id
  AND v.id = cb.variant_id
  AND cb.destination_parent_id IS NULL;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS composition_bullets_destination_order ON composition_bullets (
  composition_id,
  destination_section,
  destination_parent_type,
  destination_parent_id,
  sort_order,
  id
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS composition_bullets_usage_lookup ON composition_bullets (bullet_id, composition_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS composition_bullets_variant_usage_lookup ON composition_bullets (variant_id, composition_id);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION phase2j_destination_user (parent_type text, parent_id uuid) RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS $$
DECLARE result uuid;
BEGIN
  IF parent_type='content_item' THEN SELECT user_id INTO result FROM content_items WHERE id=parent_id;
  ELSIF parent_type='education_item' THEN SELECT user_id INTO result FROM education_items WHERE id=parent_id;
  ELSIF parent_type='tool_group' THEN SELECT user_id INTO result FROM tool_groups WHERE id=parent_id;
  ELSIF parent_type='interest' THEN SELECT user_id INTO result FROM interests WHERE id=parent_id;
  ELSIF parent_type='custom_entry' THEN SELECT user_id INTO result FROM custom_section_entries WHERE id=parent_id;
  END IF;
  RETURN result;
END $$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_phase2j_placement_ownership () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE composition_user uuid; bullet_user uuid; variant_user uuid; destination_user uuid;
BEGIN
  SELECT user_id INTO composition_user FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=NEW.composition_id;
  SELECT user_id INTO bullet_user FROM bullets WHERE id=NEW.bullet_id;
  SELECT user_id INTO variant_user FROM bullet_variants WHERE id=NEW.variant_id AND bullet_id=NEW.bullet_id;
  destination_user := phase2j_destination_user(NEW.destination_parent_type, NEW.destination_parent_id);
  IF composition_user IS NULL OR bullet_user IS NULL OR variant_user IS NULL OR destination_user IS NULL
     OR composition_user<>bullet_user OR composition_user<>variant_user OR composition_user<>destination_user THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'composition bullet ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS composition_bullets_owner_guard ON composition_bullets;

CREATE TRIGGER composition_bullets_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON composition_bullets FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_phase2j_placement_ownership ();

-- Exactly one active default remains enforced by the existing partial unique index.
-- Repair legacy bullets with active variants but no active default deterministically.
WITH
  ranked AS (
    SELECT
      id,
      bullet_id,
      row_number() OVER (
        PARTITION BY
          bullet_id
        ORDER BY
          sort_order,
          id
      ) rn
    FROM
      bullet_variants
    WHERE
      archived_at IS NULL
  ),
  missing AS (
    SELECT
      r.id
    FROM
      ranked r
    WHERE
      r.rn = 1
      AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT
          1
        FROM
          bullet_variants d
        WHERE
          d.bullet_id = r.bullet_id
          AND d.archived_at IS NULL
          AND d.is_default
      )
  )
UPDATE bullet_variants
SET
  is_default = TRUE,
  updated_at = now()
WHERE
  id IN (
    SELECT
      id
    FROM
      missing
  );

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enrich_composition_revision_snapshot () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE composition resume_compositions%ROWTYPE;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO composition FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=NEW.composition_id;
  IF composition.id IS NULL OR composition.user_id<>NEW.user_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503';
  END IF;
  NEW.snapshot=COALESCE(NEW.snapshot,'{}'::jsonb) || jsonb_build_object(
    'name',composition.name,'description',composition.description,
    'display_title',composition.display_title,'target_company',composition.target_company,
    'target_role',composition.target_role,
    'bullet_placements',COALESCE((SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
      'id',cb.id,'bullet_id',cb.bullet_id,'variant_id',cb.variant_id,
      'destination_section',cb.destination_section,'destination_parent_type',cb.destination_parent_type,
      'destination_parent_id',cb.destination_parent_id,'sort_order',cb.sort_order,
      'wording_snapshot',cb.wording_snapshot,'snapshot_source_updated_at',cb.snapshot_source_updated_at
    ) ORDER BY cb.destination_section,cb.destination_parent_id,cb.sort_order,cb.id)
    FROM composition_bullets cb WHERE cb.composition_id=NEW.composition_id),'[]'::jsonb)
  );
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

-- Enrich existing snapshots with the deterministic relationship state available at migration time.
UPDATE composition_revisions r
SET
  snapshot = r.snapshot || jsonb_build_object(
    'bullet_placements',
    COALESCE(
      (
        SELECT
          jsonb_agg(
            jsonb_build_object(
              'id',
              cb.id,
              'bullet_id',
              cb.bullet_id,
              'variant_id',
              cb.variant_id,
              'destination_section',
              cb.destination_section,
              'destination_parent_type',
              cb.destination_parent_type,
              'destination_parent_id',
              cb.destination_parent_id,
              'sort_order',
              cb.sort_order,
              'wording_snapshot',
              cb.wording_snapshot,
              'snapshot_source_updated_at',
              cb.snapshot_source_updated_at
            )
            ORDER BY
              cb.destination_section,
              cb.destination_parent_id,
              cb.sort_order,
              cb.id
          )
        FROM
          composition_bullets cb
        WHERE
          cb.composition_id = r.composition_id
      ),
      '[]'::jsonb
    )
  );

-- Permit canonical Library bullets without descriptive parent context while retaining ownership checks.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_matching_user_ownership () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE expected_user uuid;
BEGIN
  IF TG_TABLE_NAME='tools' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM tool_groups WHERE id=NEW.group_id;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='bullets' THEN
    IF NEW.parent_id IS NULL THEN expected_user:=NEW.user_id;
    ELSE SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM content_items WHERE id=NEW.parent_id;
    END IF;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='bullet_variants' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM bullets WHERE id=NEW.bullet_id;
  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='seed_imports' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=NEW.composition_id;
  END IF;
  IF expected_user IS NULL OR expected_user<>NEW.user_id THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503'; END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$;

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/migrations/010_phase2j_reusable_bullet_placements.sql

BEGIN FILE: backend/migrations/011_phase2k_library_management.sql
================================================================================

-- Phase 2K normalized tags, additive and non-destructive.
CREATE TABLE library_tags (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES app_users (id),
  display_name text NOT NULL CHECK (btrim(display_name) <> ''),
  normalized_name text NOT NULL CHECK (btrim(normalized_name) <> ''),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, normalized_name)
);

CREATE TABLE library_bullet_tags (
  bullet_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES bullets (id),
  tag_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES library_tags (id),
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (bullet_id, tag_id)
);

CREATE INDEX library_tags_user_search ON library_tags (user_id, normalized_name, id);

CREATE INDEX library_bullet_tags_tag_lookup ON library_bullet_tags (tag_id, bullet_id);

CREATE INDEX library_bullet_tags_bullet_lookup ON library_bullet_tags (bullet_id, tag_id);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_library_bullet_tag_ownership () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$ DECLARE bu uuid;tu uuid;BEGIN SELECT user_id INTO bu FROM bullets WHERE id=NEW.bullet_id;SELECT user_id INTO tu FROM library_tags WHERE id=NEW.tag_id;IF bu IS NULL OR tu IS NULL OR bu<>tu THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'library tag ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503';END IF;RETURN NEW;END $$;

CREATE TRIGGER library_bullet_tags_owner_guard
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON library_bullet_tags FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_library_bullet_tag_ownership ();

WITH
  vals AS (
    SELECT
      ci.user_id,
      b.id bullet_id,
      btrim(x) display_name,
      lower(btrim(x)) normalized_name
    FROM
      content_items ci
      JOIN bullets b ON b.parent_id = ci.id
      AND b.user_id = ci.user_id
      CROSS JOIN LATERAL unnest(ci.tags) x
    WHERE
      btrim(x) <> ''
  ),
  chosen AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT
      ON (user_id, normalized_name) user_id,
      normalized_name,
      display_name
    FROM
      vals
    ORDER BY
      user_id,
      normalized_name,
      display_name COLLATE "C"
  )
INSERT INTO
  library_tags (user_id, normalized_name, display_name)
SELECT
  user_id,
  normalized_name,
  display_name
FROM
  chosen
ON CONFLICT (user_id, normalized_name) DO NOTHING;

WITH
  vals AS (
    SELECT
      ci.user_id,
      b.id bullet_id,
      lower(btrim(x)) normalized_name
    FROM
      content_items ci
      JOIN bullets b ON b.parent_id = ci.id
      AND b.user_id = ci.user_id
      CROSS JOIN LATERAL unnest(ci.tags) x
    WHERE
      btrim(x) <> ''
  )
INSERT INTO
  library_bullet_tags (bullet_id, tag_id)
SELECT DISTINCT
  v.bullet_id,
  t.id
FROM
  vals v
  JOIN library_tags t ON t.user_id = v.user_id
  AND t.normalized_name = v.normalized_name
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/migrations/011_phase2k_library_management.sql
================================================================================

BEGIN FILE: backend/migrations/012_phase2l_date_sorting_metadata.sql

-- Phase 2L: additive composition preferences and structured custom-entry dates.
ALTER TABLE resume_compositions
ADD COLUMN preferred_page_length integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (preferred_page_length IN (1, 2, 3)),
ADD COLUMN date_format text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'long' CHECK (
  date_format IN ('year', 'numeric', 'short', 'long')
),
ADD COLUMN section_sort_modes jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb;

ALTER TABLE resume_compositions
ADD CONSTRAINT resume_compositions_name_trimmed CHECK (btrim(name) <> ''),
ADD CONSTRAINT resume_compositions_name_length CHECK (length(name) <= 120);

ALTER TABLE custom_section_entries
ADD COLUMN start_year integer CHECK (start_year BETWEEN 1900 AND 2200),
ADD COLUMN start_month integer CHECK (start_month BETWEEN 1 AND 12),
ADD COLUMN end_year integer CHECK (end_year BETWEEN 1900 AND 2200),
ADD COLUMN end_month integer CHECK (end_month BETWEEN 1 AND 12),
ADD COLUMN is_current boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE;

ALTER TABLE content_items
ADD CONSTRAINT content_items_month_requires_year CHECK (
  (
    start_month IS NULL
    OR start_year IS NOT NULL
  )
  AND (
    end_month IS NULL
    OR end_year IS NOT NULL
  )
);

ALTER TABLE content_items
ADD CONSTRAINT content_items_current_has_no_end CHECK (
  NOT is_current
  OR (
    end_year IS NULL
    AND end_month IS NULL
  )
);

ALTER TABLE custom_section_entries
ADD CONSTRAINT custom_entries_month_requires_year CHECK (
  (
    start_month IS NULL
    OR start_year IS NOT NULL
  )
  AND (
    end_month IS NULL
    OR end_year IS NOT NULL
  )
);

ALTER TABLE custom_section_entries
ADD CONSTRAINT custom_entries_current_has_no_end CHECK (
  NOT is_current
  OR (
    end_year IS NULL
    AND end_month IS NULL
  )
);

CREATE INDEX resume_compositions_home_search ON resume_compositions (
  user_id,
  archived_at,
  lower(name),
  lower(display_title),
  lower(target_company),
  lower(target_role)
);

UPDATE composition_revisions r
SET
  snapshot = r.snapshot || jsonb_build_object(
    'preferred_page_length',
    c.preferred_page_length,
    'date_format',
    c.date_format,
    'section_sort_modes',
    c.section_sort_modes
  )
FROM
  resume_compositions c
WHERE
  c.id = r.composition_id;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enrich_composition_revision_snapshot () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
DECLARE c resume_compositions%ROWTYPE;
BEGIN
 SELECT * INTO c FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=NEW.composition_id;
 IF c.id IS NULL OR c.user_id<>NEW.user_id THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503'; END IF;
 NEW.snapshot=COALESCE(NEW.snapshot,'{}'::jsonb)||jsonb_build_object('name',c.name,'description',c.description,'display_title',c.display_title,'target_company',c.target_company,'target_role',c.target_role,'preferred_page_length',c.preferred_page_length,'date_format',c.date_format,'section_sort_modes',c.section_sort_modes,'bullet_placements',COALESCE((SELECT jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(cb) ORDER BY cb.destination_section,cb.destination_parent_id,cb.sort_order,cb.id) FROM composition_bullets cb WHERE cb.composition_id=NEW.composition_id),'[]'::jsonb));
 RETURN NEW;
END $$;

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/migrations/012_phase2l_date_sorting_metadata.sql

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: backend/migrations/013_phase3b_placement_backfill.sql
================================================================================

-- Phase 3b additive placement backfill. Existing rendered wording is preserved.
INSERT INTO
  composition_bullets (
    composition_id,
    bullet_id,
    variant_id,
    destination_section,
    destination_parent_type,
    destination_parent_id,
    sort_order,
    wording_snapshot,
    snapshot_source_updated_at
  )
SELECT
  ci.composition_id,
  b.id,
  v.id,
  ci.section,
  'content_item',
  ci.item_id,
  b.sort_order,
  rendered.wording,
  v.updated_at
FROM
  composition_items ci
  JOIN resume_compositions c ON c.id = ci.composition_id
  JOIN content_items x ON x.id = ci.item_id
  JOIN bullets b ON b.parent_id = x.id
  AND b.user_id = x.user_id
  JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT
      id,
      body,
      updated_at
    FROM
      bullet_variants
    WHERE
      bullet_id = b.id
      AND archived_at IS NULL
    ORDER BY
      is_default DESC,
      sort_order,
      id
    LIMIT
      1
  ) v ON TRUE
  JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT
      COALESCE(
        c.render_payload -> ci.section -> (ci.sort_order) -> 'bullets' ->> b.sort_order,
        v.body
      ) wording
  ) rendered ON TRUE
WHERE
  ci.section IN ('projects', 'work_experience', 'leadership')
  AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT
      1
    FROM
      composition_bullets cb
    WHERE
      cb.composition_id = ci.composition_id
      AND cb.destination_parent_id = ci.item_id
  )
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/migrations/013_phase3b_placement_backfill.sql
================================================================================

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: backend/migrations/014_phase3c1_bullet_ownership.sql
================================================================================

-- Phase 3c.1 additive bullet ownership expansion.
ALTER TABLE bullets
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS education_item_id uuid REFERENCES education_items (id),
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS tool_group_id uuid REFERENCES tool_groups (id),
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS interest_id uuid REFERENCES interests (id),
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS custom_entry_id uuid REFERENCES custom_section_entries (id),
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS parent_type text GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
  CASE
    WHEN parent_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'content_item'
    WHEN education_item_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'education_item'
    WHEN tool_group_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'tool_group'
    WHEN interest_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'interest'
    WHEN custom_entry_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'custom_entry'
    ELSE ''
  END
) STORED;

ALTER TABLE bullets
ADD CONSTRAINT bullets_at_most_one_parent CHECK (
  num_nonnulls (
    parent_id,
    education_item_id,
    tool_group_id,
    interest_id,
    custom_entry_id
  ) <= 1
);

CREATE INDEX bullets_education_order ON bullets (education_item_id, sort_order);

CREATE INDEX bullets_tool_group_order ON bullets (tool_group_id, sort_order);

CREATE INDEX bullets_interest_order ON bullets (interest_id, sort_order);

CREATE INDEX bullets_custom_entry_order ON bullets (custom_entry_id, sort_order);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION phase3c1_parent_label (u uuid, t text, p uuid) RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE AS $$ DECLARE r text; BEGIN IF t='content_item' THEN SELECT CASE WHEN kind='project' THEN name ELSE concat_ws(' – ',organization,title) END INTO r FROM content_items WHERE id=p AND user_id=u; ELSIF t='education_item' THEN SELECT institution INTO r FROM education_items WHERE id=p AND user_id=u; ELSIF t='tool_group' THEN SELECT name INTO r FROM tool_groups WHERE id=p AND user_id=u; ELSIF t='interest' THEN SELECT body INTO r FROM interests WHERE id=p AND user_id=u; ELSIF t='custom_entry' THEN SELECT primary_text INTO r FROM custom_section_entries WHERE id=p AND user_id=u; END IF; RETURN r; END $$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_matching_user_ownership () RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$ DECLARE expected_user uuid; BEGIN IF TG_TABLE_NAME='tools' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM tool_groups WHERE id=NEW.group_id; ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='bullets' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM content_items WHERE id=NEW.parent_id UNION ALL SELECT user_id FROM education_items WHERE id=NEW.education_item_id UNION ALL SELECT user_id FROM tool_groups WHERE id=NEW.tool_group_id UNION ALL SELECT user_id FROM interests WHERE id=NEW.interest_id UNION ALL SELECT user_id FROM custom_section_entries WHERE id=NEW.custom_entry_id LIMIT 1; expected_user:=COALESCE(expected_user,NEW.user_id); ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='bullet_variants' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM bullets WHERE id=NEW.bullet_id; ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME='seed_imports' THEN SELECT user_id INTO expected_user FROM resume_compositions WHERE id=NEW.composition_id; END IF; IF expected_user IS NULL OR expected_user<>NEW.user_id THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'ownership mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503'; END IF; RETURN NEW; END $$;

================================================================================
END FILE: backend/migrations/014_phase3c1_bullet_ownership.sql
================================================================================

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public status: number,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export async function api<T>(
  url: string,
  options: RequestInit = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const r = await fetch(url, {
    ...options,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...(options.headers || {}) },
  });
  if (!r.ok) {
    const x = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new ApiError(x.detail || `Request failed: ${r.status}`, r.status);
  }
  return r.status === 204 ? (undefined as T) : r.json();
}

END FILE: frontend/src/api.ts
