BEGIN FILE: frontend/src/EditorHistory.ts
================================================================================

export type CommandState = "idle" | "busy" | "error";

export type EditorCommand = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  compositionId: string;
  sessionId: string;
  preRevision: number;
  identity: Record<string, string | number | boolean | null>;
  undo: () => Promise<void>;
  redo: () => Promise<void>;
};

export class EditorHistory {
  private undoStack: EditorCommand[] = [];
  private redoStack: EditorCommand[] = [];
  private state: CommandState = "idle";
  private compositionId = "";
  private sessionId = "";
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  subscribe(listener: () => void) {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  }
  private notify() {
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
  }
  begin(compositionId: string, sessionId: string) {
    if (this.compositionId !== compositionId || this.sessionId !== sessionId) {
      this.compositionId = compositionId;
      this.sessionId = sessionId;
      this.clear();
    }
  }
  clear() {
    this.undoStack = [];
    this.redoStack = [];
    this.state = "idle";
    this.notify();
  }
  record(command: EditorCommand) {
    if (
      command.compositionId !== this.compositionId ||
      command.sessionId !== this.sessionId
    )
      return;
    if (!command.id || !Object.keys(command.identity).length)
      throw new Error("Undo command requires durable identity.");
    this.undoStack.push(command);
    this.redoStack = [];
    this.notify();
  }
  private async run(
    source: EditorCommand[],
    destination: EditorCommand[],
    direction: "undo" | "redo",
  ) {
    if (this.state === "busy") return;
    const command = source[source.length - 1];
    if (!command) return;
    this.state = "busy";
    this.notify();
    try {
      await command[direction]();
      source.pop();
      destination.push(command);
      this.state = "idle";
    } catch (error) {
      this.state = "error";
      throw error;
    } finally {
      this.notify();
    }
  }
  undo() {
    return this.run(this.undoStack, this.redoStack, "undo");
  }
  redo() {
    return this.run(this.redoStack, this.undoStack, "redo");
  }
  get snapshot() {
    return {
      canUndo: this.undoStack.length > 0 && this.state !== "busy",
      canRedo: this.redoStack.length > 0 && this.state !== "busy",
      busy: this.state === "busy",
      state: this.state,
    };
  }
}

BEGIN FILE: frontend/src/FitIndicator.tsx

import React from "react";
export type Fit = {
  fits: boolean;
  actualPageCount?: number;
  preferredPageCount?: number;
  state?: string;
  pages?: any[];
  message?: string;
};
export default function FitIndicator({
  fit,
  compact = false,
}: {
  fit: Fit | null;
  compact?: boolean;
}) {
  if (!fit)
    return (
      <div className={`fit pending ${compact ? "compact" : ""}`} role="status">
        ⏳ Measuring…
      </div>
    );
  if (fit.state === "measurement-error")
    return (
      <div className={`fit bad ${compact ? "compact" : ""}`} role="alert">
        ⚠ {compact ? "Couldn’t measure" : "Couldn’t measure the resume."}
      </div>
    );
  const a = fit.actualPageCount || 1,
    p = fit.preferredPageCount || 1,
    label = fit.fits
      ? `Fits preferred length (${a} of ${p} ${p === 1 ? "page" : "pages"}).`
      : `Exceeds preferred length by ${Math.max(1, a - p)} ${a - p === 1 ? "page" : "pages"}. Export is blocked.`;
  return (
    <div
      className={`fit ${fit.fits ? "ok" : "bad"} ${compact ? "compact" : ""}`}
      role="status"
      aria-label={label}
    >
      {fit.fits ? "✓" : "⚠"}{" "}
      {compact
        ? fit.fits
          ? `Fits ${p} ${p === 1 ? "page" : "pages"}`
          : `${a} of ${p} pages`
        : label}
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from "react";
import ModalShell from "./ModalShell";
export function RenameResumeDialog({ item, close, save }: any) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(item.name),
    [error, setError] = useState("");
  return (
    <ModalShell title="Rename resume" close={close}>
      <label>
        Internal Name
        <input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
      </label>
      {error && <div role="alert">{error}</div>}
      <div className="form-actions">
        <button onClick={close}>Cancel</button>
        <button
          className="primary"
          onClick={() =>
            name.trim()
              ? save({ ...item, name: name.trim() }, setError)
              : setError("Internal name is required.")
          }
        >
          Save
        </button>
      </div>
    </ModalShell>
  );
}
export function EditDetailsDialog({ item, close, save }: any) {
  const [x, setX] = useState({ ...item }),
    [error, setError] = useState("");
  const f = (k: string, l: string) => (
    <label>
      {l}
      <input
        value={x[k] || ""}
        onChange={(e) => setX({ ...x, [k]: e.target.value })}
      />
    </label>
  );
  return (
    <ModalShell title="Edit details" close={close}>
      <div className="form-grid">
        {f("name", "Internal Name")}
        {f("display_title", "Display Title")}
        {f("target_company", "Target Company")}
        {f("target_role", "Target Role")}
      </div>
      {error && <div role="alert">{error}</div>}
      <div className="form-actions">
        <button onClick={close}>Cancel</button>
        <button
          className="primary"
          onClick={() =>
            x.name.trim()
              ? save(x, setError)
              : setError("Internal name is required.")
          }
        >
          Save
        </button>
      </div>
    </ModalShell>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import MonthYearField from "./MonthYearField";

export type LibraryEntryKind =
  | "employment"
  | "project"
  | "education"
  | "leadership"
  | "tool-group"
  | "interest"
  | "custom-section"
  | "custom-entry";
export const blankDrafts: Record<LibraryEntryKind, any> = {
  employment: {
    kind: "employment",
    organization: "",
    title: "",
    location: "",
    date_label: "",
    start_year: null,
    start_month: null,
    end_year: null,
    end_month: null,
    is_current: false,
    bullets: [],
  },
  project: {
    kind: "project",
    name: "",
    subtitle: "",
    location: "",
    date_label: "",
    start_year: null,
    start_month: null,
    end_year: null,
    end_month: null,
    is_current: false,
    bullets: [],
  },
  education: {
    institution: "",
    college_school: "",
    location: "",
    degree_type: "",
    degree_major: "",
    graduation_year: null,
    graduation_month: null,
    display_date: "",
  },
  leadership: {
    kind: "leadership",
    organization: "",
    title: "",
    location: "",
    date_label: "",
    start_year: null,
    start_month: null,
    end_year: null,
    end_month: null,
    is_current: false,
    bullets: [],
  },
  "tool-group": { name: "" },
  interest: { body: "" },
  "custom-section": { title: "", layout: "list" },
  "custom-entry": {
    section_id: "",
    primary_text: "",
    secondary_text: "",
    location: "",
    date_label: "",
    start_year: null,
    start_month: null,
    end_year: null,
    end_month: null,
    is_current: false,
  },
};
export const cloneBlankDraft = (kind: LibraryEntryKind) =>
  structuredClone(blankDrafts[kind]);
export function LibraryEntryForm({
  kind,
  draft,
  setDraft,
  error,
}: {
  kind: LibraryEntryKind;
  draft: any;
  setDraft: (x: any) => void;
  error?: string;
}) {
  const field = (name: string, label: string) => (
    <label>
      {label}
      <input
        value={draft[name] ?? ""}
        onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, [name]: e.target.value })}
      />
      {error && error.toLowerCase().includes(name.replaceAll("_", " ")) ? (
        <span role="alert">{error}</span>
      ) : null}
    </label>
  );
  const dates = (
    <>
      <MonthYearField
        label="Start"
        year={draft.start_year}
        month={draft.start_month}
        onChange={({ year, month }) =>
          setDraft({ ...draft, start_year: year, start_month: month })
        }
      />
      <MonthYearField
        label="End"
        year={draft.end_year}
        month={draft.end_month}
        disabled={!!draft.is_current}
        onChange={({ year, month }) =>
          setDraft({ ...draft, end_year: year, end_month: month })
        }
      />
      <label className="choice">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          checked={!!draft.is_current}
          onChange={(e) =>
            setDraft({
              ...draft,
              is_current: e.target.checked,
              end_year: e.target.checked ? null : draft.end_year,
              end_month: e.target.checked ? null : draft.end_month,
            })
          }
        />
        Current / ongoing
      </label>
      {field("date_label", "Display-date override")}
    </>
  );
  if (kind === "employment" || kind === "leadership")
    return (
      <div className="form-grid">
        {field("organization", "Organization")}
        {field(
          "title",
          kind === "leadership" ? "Leadership title" : "Job title",
        )}
        {field("location", "Location")}
        {dates}
      </div>
    );
  if (kind === "project")
    return (
      <div className="form-grid">
        {field("name", "Project name")}
        {field("subtitle", "Subtitle")}
        {field("location", "Location")}
        {dates}
      </div>
    );
  if (kind === "education")
    return (
      <div className="form-grid">
        {field("institution", "Institution")}
        {field("college_school", "College or school")}
        {field("degree_type", "Degree type")}
        {field("degree_major", "Degree or major")}
        {field("location", "Location")}
        <MonthYearField
          label="Graduation"
          year={draft.graduation_year}
          month={draft.graduation_month}
          onChange={({ year, month }) =>
            setDraft({
              ...draft,
              graduation_year: year,
              graduation_month: month,
            })
          }
        />
        {field("display_date", "Display-date override")}
      </div>
    );
  if (kind === "tool-group")
    return <div className="form-grid">{field("name", "Group name")}</div>;
  if (kind === "interest")
    return <div className="form-grid">{field("body", "Interest")}</div>;
  if (kind === "custom-section")
    return (
      <div className="form-grid">
        {field("title", "Section title")}
        <label>
          Layout
          <select
            value={draft.layout}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, layout: e.target.value })}
          >
            {["list", "compact", "dated", "experience"].map((x) => (
              <option key={x}>{x}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>
    );
  return (
    <div className="form-grid">
      {field("primary_text", "Primary text")}
      {field("secondary_text", "Secondary text")}
      {field("location", "Location")}
      {dates}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: frontend/src/ModalShell.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useId, useRef } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";

type ModalShellProps = {
  title: string;
  close: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  variant?: "dialog" | "side-panel";
  className?: string;
};
const FOCUSABLE =
  'button:not([disabled]),input:not([disabled]),select:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),a[href],[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';
export default function ModalShell({
  title,
  close,
  children,
  variant = "dialog",
  className = "",
}: ModalShellProps) {
  const titleId = useId();
  const panel = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const opener = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    const inertTarget =
      variant === "side-panel"
        ? document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".editing-shell .editor")
        : document.querySelector<HTMLElement>("#root");
    inertTarget?.setAttribute("inert", "");
    requestAnimationFrame(() =>
      panel.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE)?.focus(),
    );
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        event.preventDefault();
        close();
        return;
      }
      if (event.key !== "Tab" || !panel.current) return;
      const items = [...panel.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE)];
      if (!items.length) return;
      const first = items[0],
        last = items.at(-1)!;
      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      }
      if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };
    addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      inertTarget?.removeAttribute("inert");
      opener?.focus();
    };
  }, [close, variant]);
  return createPortal(
    <div className={`modal-backdrop ${variant}`} role="presentation">
      <div
        ref={panel}
        className={`modal-shell ${variant} ${className}`}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal={variant === "dialog" ? "true" : undefined}
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
      >
        <div className="modal-heading">
          <h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2>
          <button type="button" aria-label={`Close ${title}`} onClick={close}>
            ×
          </button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}

END FILE: frontend/src/ModalShell.tsx

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: frontend/src/MonthYearField.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";

type Props = {
  label: string;
  year?: number | string | null;
  month?: number | string | null;
  disabled?: boolean;
  onChange: (value: { year: number | null; month: number | null }) => void;
};
export default function MonthYearField({
  label,
  year,
  month,
  disabled,
  onChange,
}: Props) {
  const initialYearOnly = !!year && !month;
  const [yearOnly, setYearOnly] = useState(initialYearOnly);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const supportsMonth = useMemo(() => {
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    input.type = "month";
    return input.type === "month";
  }, []);
  const value = year
    ? month
      ? `${String(year).padStart(4, "0")}-${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}`
      : String(year)
    : "";
  const fallback =
    month && year
      ? `${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}/${year}`
      : year
        ? String(year)
        : "";
  const parse = (raw: string) => {
    if (!raw) {
      setError("");
      onChange({ year: null, month: null });
      return;
    }
    const match = raw.match(/^(?:(0[1-9]|1[0-2])\/)?(\d{4})$/);
    if (!match) {
      setError("Enter a year (2024) or month/year (03/2024).");
      return;
    }
    setError("");
    onChange({
      year: Number(match[2]),
      month: match[1] ? Number(match[1]) : null,
    });
  };
  const id = `month-year-${label.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")}`;
  return (
    <div className="month-year-field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      <label className="choice">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          checked={yearOnly}
          disabled={disabled}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setYearOnly(e.target.checked);
            onChange({ year: year ? Number(year) : null, month: null });
          }}
        />
        Year only {label}
      </label>
      {yearOnly ? (
        <input
          id={id}
          inputMode="numeric"
          pattern="\d{4}"
          value={year || ""}
          disabled={disabled}
          aria-describedby={error ? `${id}-error` : undefined}
          onChange={(e) =>
            onChange({
              year: e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null,
              month: null,
            })
          }
          onBlur={(e) => parse(e.target.value)}
        />
      ) : supportsMonth ? (
        <input
          id={id}
          type="month"
          value={value.includes("-") ? value : ""}
          disabled={disabled}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const [y, m] = e.target.value.split("-");
            onChange({
              year: y ? Number(y) : null,
              month: m ? Number(m) : null,
            });
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        <input
          id={id}
          type="text"
          placeholder="MM/YYYY"
          defaultValue={fallback}
          disabled={disabled}
          aria-describedby={error ? `${id}-error` : undefined}
          onBlur={(e) => parse(e.target.value)}
        />
      )}
      {error && (
        <span id={`${id}-error`} role="alert">
          {error}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

END FILE: frontend/src/MonthYearField.tsx

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: frontend/src/OverflowMenu.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
export type OverflowItem = {
  label: string;
  onSelect: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  danger?: boolean;
};
export default function OverflowMenu({
  label,
  items,
}: {
  label: string;
  items: OverflowItem[];
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false),
    root = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null),
    trigger = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const f = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!root.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", f);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", f);
  }, []);
  const focus = (i: number) => {
    const a = [
      ...(root.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>(
        '[role="menuitem"]:not([disabled])',
      ) || []),
    ];
    a[(i + a.length) % a.length]?.focus();
  };
  return (
    <div className="overflow-menu" ref={root}>
      <button
        ref={trigger}
        aria-label={label}
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}
      >
        ⋯
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div
          role="menu"
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            const a = [
                ...(root.current?.querySelectorAll('[role="menuitem"]') || []),
              ],
              i = a.indexOf(document.activeElement as Element);
            if (e.key === "ArrowDown") {
              e.preventDefault();
              focus(i + 1);
            }
            if (e.key === "ArrowUp") {
              e.preventDefault();
              focus(i - 1);
            }
            if (e.key === "Home") {
              e.preventDefault();
              focus(0);
            }
            if (e.key === "End") {
              e.preventDefault();
              focus(-1);
            }
            if (e.key === "Escape") {
              setOpen(false);
              trigger.current?.focus();
            }
          }}
        >
          {items.map((x) => (
            <button
              key={x.label}
              role="menuitem"
              disabled={x.disabled}
              className={x.danger ? "danger-text" : ""}
              onClick={() => {
                setOpen(false);
                x.onSelect();
                requestAnimationFrame(() => trigger.current?.focus());
              }}
            >
              {x.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: frontend/src/ResumePreview.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { Fit } from "./FitIndicator";
export default function ResumePreview({
  compositionId,
  revision,
  revisionId,
  preferredPageCount,
  onFit,
  currentPage,
}: {
  compositionId: string;
  revision: number;
  revisionId?: string | null;
  preferredPageCount: number;
  onFit: (x: Fit) => void;
  currentPage: number;
}) {
  const [html, setHtml] = useState(""),
    seq = useRef(0),
    frame = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = ++seq.current,
      url = revisionId
        ? `/api/compositions/${compositionId}/revisions/${revisionId}/render`
        : `/api/compositions/${compositionId}/render?revision=${revision}`;
    fetch(url)
      .then(async (r) => {
        if (!r.ok)
          throw new Error(
            (await r.json().catch(() => ({}))).detail || "Preview failed",
          );
        return r.text();
      })
      .then((t) => {
        if (id === seq.current) setHtml(t);
      })
      .catch((e) =>
        onFit({ fits: false, state: "measurement-error", message: e.message }),
      );
  }, [compositionId, revision, revisionId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const fn = (e: MessageEvent) => {
      if (
        e.source !== frame.current?.contentWindow ||
        e.data?.type !== "resume-pages"
      )
        return;
      const actual = e.data.actual_page_count || 1;
      onFit({
        fits: e.data.state === "measured" && actual <= preferredPageCount,
        actualPageCount: actual,
        preferredPageCount,
        state: e.data.state,
        pages: e.data.pages,
        message: e.data.message,
      });
    };
    addEventListener("message", fn);
    return () => removeEventListener("message", fn);
  }, [onFit, preferredPageCount]);
  useEffect(() => {
    frame.current?.contentWindow?.postMessage(
      { type: "resume-show-page", pageIndex: currentPage - 1 },
      "*",
    );
  }, [currentPage, html]);
  return (
    <iframe
      ref={frame}
      title="Live resume preview"
      className="preview"
      srcDoc={html}
    />
  );
}

================================================================================
END FILE: frontend/src/ResumePreview.tsx

================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: frontend/src/RevisionHistoryPanel.tsx
================================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import ModalShell from "./ModalShell";
import { api } from "./api";
export default function RevisionHistoryPanel({
  comp,
  close,
  preview,
  restore,
}: any) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<any[]>([]),
    [selected, setSelected] = useState(0),
    [error, setError] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    api<any[]>(`/api/compositions/${comp.id}/revisions`)
      .then(setRows)
      .catch((e: any) => setError(e.message));
  }, [comp.id, comp.revision]);
  const choose = (i: number) => {
    setSelected(i);
    if (rows[i]) preview(rows[i]);
  };
  return (
    <ModalShell title="Revision History" variant="side-panel" close={close}>
      <div
        className="revision-history"
        role="listbox"
        tabIndex={0}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === "ArrowDown") {
            e.preventDefault();
            choose(Math.min(rows.length - 1, selected + 1));
          }
          if (e.key === "ArrowUp") {
            e.preventDefault();
            choose(Math.max(0, selected - 1));
          }
        }}
      >
        {error && <div role="alert">{error}</div>}
        {rows.map((r, i) => (
          <button
            role="option"
            aria-selected={selected === i}
            key={r.id}
            onClick={() => choose(i)}
          >
            <strong>Revision {r.revision}</strong>
            <span>{new Date(r.created_at).toLocaleString()}</span>
            <span>
              {r.source}
              {r.note ? ` · ${r.note}` : ""}
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="form-actions">
        <button onClick={close}>Return to current</button>
        <button
          className="primary"
          disabled={!rows[selected]}
          onClick={() => restore(rows[selected], setError)}
        >
          Restore as new revision
        </button>
      </div>
    </ModalShell>
  );
}

BEGIN FILE: frontend/src/StandaloneLibrary.tsx
